# Corrections

### Cell-therapy manufacturing time, 12 November 2026

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-12 | [Vein-to-vein time](/terms/vein-to-vein-time/) | The summary gave unscoped regional turnaround averages and attributed cross-trial outcome differences to waiting time, without the population and comparison needed for those claims | Raw FDA January 2024 CAR T-cell guidance, sections VI.B.4 and VI.B.5, plus its distinct manufacturing, eligibility and failure-to-treat considerations | Defines the interval and its stages, separates product availability from clinical readiness, and retains participant-flow examples with their actual denominators |

The engineered-cell-therapy assessment preserves earlier contributor notes and links. Its source-specific access limits and original/later requirement inventories remain separate from main, live, expert or language acceptance.

### Targeted inhibitor mechanisms, 12 November 2026

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-12 | [EGFR](/targets/egfr/) | A retained lung note assigned sensitivity or resistance to every inhibitor generation by mutation class, including resistance of exon 20 insertions to all inhibitors; its testing sentence did not distinguish protein from DNA | Lynch 2004 and the raw FDA July 2025 sunvozertinib approval notification | Keeps the cohort frequencies, replaces the universal drug-response claims with variant-specific evidence and a dated, population-qualified accelerated-approval example, and distinguishes immunostaining from identification of a DNA alteration |
| 2026-11-12 | [BRD4](/targets/brd4/) | The account referred to an unavailable lesson figure and could make a fixed drawn acetyl mark sound like a measured unchanged state | Fresh views of both pinned figure themes and resolved JQ1 and Shu primary evidence | Explains pocket occupation, protein depletion and the fixed-mark drawing convention in self-contained prose |

The five source pairs are byte-identical across the two pins. Their requirements, source depth and draft-only verification are recorded in the targeted-small-molecule assessment packet. Older catalogue assertions outside the named corrections are not recertified by this assessment.

### Statistical questions, populations and design, 12 November 2026

The [statistics assessment](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/study-statistics-2026-11-12/README.md) retains five later-only lessons and their source-first requirements. They have no counterpart at the original pinned paths.

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-12 | [Other-cause mortality](/terms/other-cause-mortality/) | The introduction inferred dependence from mutually exclusive events and conflated cumulative-incidence estimation with Fine-Gray regression | Austin's method definitions and the Therneau, Crowson and Atkinson vignette | Separates Aalen-Johansen probabilities, cause-specific rates and subdistribution hazards; replaces the single-cause explanation of disagreement between prostate trials without recertifying the retained prostate-cohort figures |
| 2026-11-12 | [Bayesian design](/terms/bayesian-trial-design/) and [response-adaptive randomisation](/terms/response-adaptive-randomisation/) | Leads implied a model-free probability that a treatment works and guaranteed efficiency or better assignments | Original I-SPY2 methods, complete primary abstracts, FDA's 2019 final guidance and January 2026 draft | States model, prior, endpoint, future-trial success criterion and operating-characteristic conditions; distinguishes predictive graduation, exploratory survival and a person's response-guided treatment path |
| 2026-11-12 | [Non-inferiority](/terms/non-inferiority/) and [margins](/terms/non-inferiority-margin/) | Broad claims obscured margin justification, interval level and analysis-population limits | FDA's 2016 guidance and named Z0011, PERSEPHONE and EA1131 reports | Keeps M1, M2, clinical scope and confidence level explicit; distinguishes non-inferiority, equivalence, superiority and an inconclusive result |
| 2026-11-12 | [Power and sample size](/terms/sample-size-re-estimation/) | Underpowering was called the commonest reason useful treatments fail and blinded re-estimation was said to have no statistical cost | ICH E9, adaptive-design guidance, CALOR methods and power-method sources | Replaces universal claims with specified effects, event information, planning assumptions and analysis requirements; independently checks the complete fictional calculations |
| 2026-11-12 | [Estimands](/terms/estimand/), [master protocols](/terms/master-protocol/) and [seamless designs](/terms/seamless-adaptive/) | Overviews conflated analysis sets with target effects and implied general patient-number or speed advantages | Targeted ICH E9(R1) and FDA guidance passages | Separates estimand, estimator and sensitivity analysis; makes efficiency conditional. Unverified clinical catalogues are withdrawn from these overviews with complete before/after fields retained in the packet and existing links preserved |

Twenty requirements about competing risks are carried only by an unapplied proposal for the existing owner in peer draft #371. No duplicate is registered. Twenty-two stale unreviewed translations are withdrawn to English fallback and eight Simple English entries are corrected. Main, live, expert, learner and language approval remain separate.

### Imaging measurements and breast follow-up, 12 November 2026

The [imaging and follow-up assessment](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/imaging-follow-up-2026-11-12/README.md) distinguishes signal, image pattern, calculated quantity, tissue diagnosis and a useful clinical strategy. The pinned original and later inventories remain separate.

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-12 | [Breast follow-up](/protocols/breast-cancer-follow-up-after-treatment/) | The record said no later ASCO guideline was found and presented the 2013 calendar as current | Resolved 2026 ASCO abstract and official ASCO/Guideline Central practice summary | Adds risk-based intensity and the age, risk, anatomy and time conditions for mammography; preserves older quotations explicitly as history and names the original-journal access limit |
| 2026-11-12 | [MRI](/technologies/mri/), [ultrasound](/technologies/ultrasound/) and [mammography](/technologies/mammography/) | Broad leads and brief summaries did not carry the measured quantity, complete examples or detection limits | Primary MRI abstracts and targeted methods, raw official modality descriptions and complete source figures | Separates contrast, morphology, length, selected-voxel volume, acoustic images and X-ray reconstructions from viable-cell amount and sampled tissue; withdraws unsupported general rankings, screening percentages and cost/deployment catalogue claims from these leads |
| 2026-11-12 | [PET](/technologies/pet/), [FDG PET](/technologies/fdg-pet/) and [PET/CT](/technologies/pet-ct/) | Signal was described as tumour activity, localisation as exact and availability as universal | Technical EANM 2015 passages and official PET descriptions | Names tracer, background, coverage, time, uptake quantity and partial-volume limits; keeps the inaccessible 2025 revision distinct from the technical version actually read |
| 2026-11-12 | [Immuno-PET](/technologies/immuno-pet/) and [granzyme B PET](/technologies/granzyme-b-pet/) | The antibody account included a non-antibody tracer and implied any binder could provide complete target imaging | Resolved primary feasibility studies and targeted CD8/granzyme passages | Keeps antibody-derived imaging separate from peptide/peptidomimetic imaging; preserves study populations, competition, normal-organ signal and exploratory outcome scope without a scan-guided treatment claim |
| 2026-11-12 | [Theranostics](/terms/theranostics/) and [dosimetry](/technologies/radioligand-dosimetry/) | The short account lacked the complete product-bridging and unit exercises | Named PSMA guideline abstracts, targeted FDA 2019 nonclinical guidance and the source diagram | Adds vector, activity, mass, time and dose distinctions; a bright diagnostic scan or shared target does not establish a different therapeutic product's distribution, absorbed dose or clinical benefit |

All earlier notes, links and protocol quotations are preserved. Fifty-six stale unreviewed translations are withdrawn to English fallback and seven Simple English entries corrected. The source-first inventory retains one explicitly documented transcription erratum. These are draft data changes with no main, live, expert, learner or language approval.

### Reading residual-disease monitoring results, 12 November 2026

The [monitoring-guide assessment](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/residual-monitoring-guides-2026-11-12/README.md) separates a sampled blood signal, regional imaging, removed tissue and evidence for a result-guided action. The fictional report, imaging and treatment examples now have self-contained explanations in the canonical records.

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-12 | [Cell-free DNA](/terms/cfdna/) and [tumour-informed assays](/technologies/tumour-informed-assay/) | Leads gave an unqualified tumour-fraction range, product-category claims and an unnecessarily narrow tumour-reference description | Complete primary abstracts, targeted assay passages and FDA's November 2024 drug-development guidance | States the specimen mixture, reference quality, target list and intended use; keeps monitoring, profiling and screening questions separate without a universal sensitivity ranking |
| 2026-11-12 | [Residual-disease testing](/technologies/mrd-testing/) and [ctDNA units](/terms/ctdna-units/) | The lead and retained catalogue encouraged cross-method comparisons and exhaustive subclone-detection claims without their required assumptions | Specimen and assay comparisons, analytical-limit definitions and the complete fictional sampling exercises | Keeps quantities, calibration, input and calling rules together; separates pooled signal detection from validated clone measurement and below-range detection from an exact fold change |
| 2026-11-12 | [Blood and imaging discordance](/terms/ctdna-imaging-discordance/) | The short account did not carry the complete measurement comparison or worked residual-tissue course | Primary MRI methods, raw modality definitions and NRG-BR005's actual complete or near-complete imaging eligibility | Explains regional contrast-dependent features, finite blood sampling, all four interpretation patterns and why favourable measurements do not establish tissue pCR or a useful treatment change |
| 2026-11-12 | [Clonal haematopoiesis](/terms/clonal-haematopoiesis/) | The plasma-origin sentence treated a method category as sufficient to resolve false positives | Shah's named tumour cohort and the Bolton and Razavi studies at their recorded reading depth | Requires origin assessment within tumour and blood-cell coverage and sensitivity; preserves ambiguous assignments and distinguishes compartments from a gene name alone |
| 2026-11-12 | [Magbanua's I-SPY2 ctDNA analysis](/papers/paper-magbanua-ispy2-ctdna-neoadjuvant-ann-oncol-2021/) | The account blurred the 84-person cohort with the 60-person joint post-treatment analysis and implied equivalence from similar observed outcomes | Primary abstract and targeted methods and analysis passages | Retains 291 available plasma samples, the 17 pCR and 43 non-pCR groups, both hazard-ratio intervals and the absence of a randomised blood-guided strategy; the exact denominator behind the reported final-draw 9% remains an explicit audit gap |

Eight stale unreviewed cfDNA translations are withdrawn to English fallback and one Simple English entry is corrected. Earlier links and notes remain apart from two explicitly recorded MRD note corrections. No routine testing interval, individual treatment decision, independent expert review, language approval, main coverage or deployment is implied.

### Treatment setting and the early-TNBC sequence, 12 November 2026

The [treatment-setting assessment](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/early-tnbc-sequencing-2026-11-12/README.md) keeps the treatment strategy, its component jobs, the surgical findings and the next evidence question separate. Historical treatment comparisons do not establish the benefit of adding those treatments to pembrolizumab.

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-12 | [Lines of therapy](/terms/first-line/) and [neoadjuvant/adjuvant](/terms/neoadjuvant-adjuvant/) | The leads restricted first-line to advanced cancer and made timing labels a simple before/after rule; retained prose generalised treatment ranking and line counting | Raw NCI definitions, the KEYNOTE-355 exposure variables and comparison of the complete treatment sequence | States the disease setting and counting convention, separates treatment role from calendar position and withdraws unsupported universal ranking rules |
| 2026-11-12 | [De-escalation](/terms/de-escalation/) and [chemotherapy regimen](/terms/chemotherapy-regimen/) | The account conflated a response-guided research rule with proven safe omission, and described KEYNOTE-522 as residual-only escalation | I-SPY2.2 abstract, KEYNOTE-522 reports and label, and the complete fictional rule and calendar exercises | Makes the input, threshold, action, outcome and safety boundary explicit; preserves the whole perioperative assignment and the distinct jobs, doses, intervals and support in a regimen |
| 2026-11-12 | [Residual TNBC](/terms/tnbc-residual-disease-decision/), [c-TRAK TN](/trials/c-trak-tn/) and [BRE12-158](/trials/bre12-158/) | A ctDNA percentage used the wrong denominator; the c-TRAK lead inferred that detection was too late to prevent relapse, and residual burden sounded sufficient to choose a medicine | Radovich's 142-person ctDNA cohort, Turner's detection/staging/treatment groups and the randomised BRE12-158 report | Keeps assay populations, imaging findings and the small treated subset separate; distinguishes prognostic association from useful treatment and retains eligibility, local-care and delivery questions |
| 2026-11-12 | [EA1131](/trials/ea1131/) and [KEYNOTE-355](/trials/keynote-355/) | EA1131 was described as unindexed, its result became a universal treatment rule, and KEYNOTE-355's registry total was explained using an incorrect 32-person run-in | Live publication identities, primary abstracts and registry participant-flow tables | Separates EA1131's 410 randomised participants from 415 registry enrolments; KEYNOTE-355 lists 35 part-1 starters and 847 part-2 randomisations, matching 882 enrolments; neither trial establishes a universal rule outside its question |
| 2026-11-12 | [OlympiA](/trials/olympia/), [CREATE-X](/trials/create-x/) and [neoadjuvant versus adjuvant breast treatment](/terms/neoadjuvant-versus-adjuvant-breast/) | Leads overgeneralised eligibility, subgroup ranking or timing equivalence; the B-51 account turned a nonsignificant comparison into proof of no benefit | Primary trial abstracts, targeted OlympiA eligibility text, the historical chemotherapy meta-analysis and B-51 report | Retains studied populations, confidence intervals and follow-up; separates historical same-chemotherapy timing from modern strategies and local-treatment questions |
| 2026-11-12 | [pCR](/terms/pcr/), [perioperative immune window](/terms/perioperative-immune-window/), [ASCENT-05](/trials/ascent-05/) and [TROPION-Breast03](/trials/tropion-breast03/) | The retained CTNeoBC associations lacked intervals, the review timeframe was omitted and two registry update dates were stale | Resolved CTNeoBC and Horowitz abstracts and current registry records | Adds the reported intervals and review-specific days-to-weeks framing; updates registry dates without presenting unposted results as treatment evidence |

Thirty-two obsolete unreviewed translations are withdrawn to English fallback and five Simple English entries corrected. Earlier notes and links remain apart from five explicitly recorded note corrections. A later-only follow-up definition is assessed separately from the still-unreviewed surveillance lesson. No independent expert, learner, language, main or live approval is implied.

### Reading trial evidence and analysis populations, 12 November 2026

The [cancer-evidence guide assessment](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/evidence-reading-guides-2026-11-12/README.md) keeps study questions, analysis populations, event definitions and dated results together. The original four-lesson and later five-lesson inventories remain separate.

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-12 | [Intention to treat](/terms/intention-to-treat/) | The account overstated real-world representativeness, defined modified and per-protocol sets too narrowly, treated evaluable analyses as necessarily inflated and asserted a site-wide default without verification | ICH E9 analysis-set and safety sections and the E9(R1) estimand framework | Preserves assignment while qualifying exclusions, missing outcomes, design and sensitivity analysis; withdraws the unsupported site-wide default and uses an explicit fictional denominator example |
| 2026-11-12 | [Endpoint](/terms/endpoint/) | Meeting a primary endpoint was defined only as beating a comparator, and secondary outcomes were categorically described as not powered | ICH trial-design guidance, CONSORT reporting and FDA's December 2018 endpoint guidance | Refers to the actual planned question and testing rule; separates event definitions, clocks, composite components and context-dependent surrogate evidence |
| 2026-11-12 | [Data maturity](/terms/data-maturity/) | Unreached medians were conflated with unreliable treatment comparisons, censoring was reduced to event-free cutoff observations and regulatory follow-up requirements were generalised | Primary KEYNOTE-522 abstracts, registry analyses, NIST methods and ICH guidance | Distinguishes median outcome, follow-up, event information and precision; removes the universal regulatory assertion and retains the named trial's planned analyses |
| 2026-11-12 | [KEYNOTE-522](/trials/keynote-522/) | The lead made an unsupported survival superlative; response cohorts and dates were compressed, and exploratory residual-disease strata were described without their uncertainty | Primary reports, the label, sponsor-posted registry and the authors' 2026 conference abstract; Pusztai's 2024 exploratory abstract | States the whole assigned strategy, separates first-602, first-1,002 and all-1,174 response analyses, keeps each survival cutoff and preserves the seven-year figures with a primary conference citation; qualifies post-treatment subgroup estimates |
| 2026-11-12 | [OptimICE-pCR](/trials/optimice-pcr/) | The summary described a further year of postoperative immunotherapy and implied that pCR meant all cancer had disappeared | The current registry specifies the postoperative randomisation, response requirement and 27-week comparison | States the actual continuation-versus-observation question, non-inferiority endpoint, duration and recruiting state without presenting safe omission as established |
| 2026-11-12 | [Trial protocol](/terms/trial-protocol/) | A categorical sentence treated lack of a traceable prespecified analysis as enough to classify every result as merely a hypothesis | ICH and CONSORT distinguish evidence role, planning, relevant outcome access and reporting | Requests the dated plan and access history, distinguishing confirmatory questions, planned exploration and later discoveries rather than classifying a result from its p-value alone |

Forty-eight obsolete unreviewed translations across six short summaries are withdrawn to the English fallback, including response-rate wording that survived an earlier canonical correction. Six Simple English entries are corrected. Earlier notes and links remain, apart from the explicitly qualified exploratory subgroup sentence. One detailed I-SPY2 control clause stays source-open and earns no coverage credit. No independent expert, learner, language, main or live approval is implied.

Test quality, clinical meaning, treatment benefit and product readiness remain separate questions.

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-12 | [Analytical and clinical validation](/terms/analytical-vs-clinical-validation/) | The lead limited clinical validity to predicting a patient outcome, while the summary implied one universal validation ladder | MedlinePlus Genetics separates detecting a variant, its relationship to disease presence or risk, and useful clinical information | States three intended-use questions and distinguishes reliable measurement, clinical meaning and consequences of a decision |
| 2026-11-12 | [External validation](/terms/external-validation/) | The lead defined external validation through a different hospital, country or platform and called it the only evidence that a result travels; the summary favoured other investigators/platforms and dismissed internal evidence as no result | TRIPOD-Cluster explanation, Box 1, distinguishes apparent, internal and external evaluation and includes later-period data from the same investigators | States the evaluation question and its limits, preserves the value of internal resampling and removes a universal pass/fail implication |
| 2026-11-12 | External validation, retained benchmark assertions | Routine TCGA-to-METABRIC testing and a typical concordance-index decline were asserted without primary verification here | Source and field comparison | Intentionally withdraws those generalisations rather than calling them false; retains the separately sourced Michiels seven-study result at its historical scope |

Earlier notes, source links and the separate peer-draft versions remain. No translation review, expert or learner sign-off, main coverage or deployment is implied.

Source-first requirements, precise source-reading limits and field evidence remain separate from expert or language approval.

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-11 | [Neoantigen](/terms/neoantigen/) | Novel tumour sequence was said to guarantee an immune target that could be attacked without harming normal cells | TESLA primary prediction/validation methods and the separate presentation, recognition and safety questions | Removes the blanket safety claim, adds a candidate-evidence framework, withdraws eight obsolete unreviewed translations and corrects Simple English |
| 2026-11-11 | [Cross-presentation](/terms/dendritic-cell-cross-presentation/) | The opening confused tumour-cell class-I display with naive CD8 priming and described a single intracellular route as universal | Cross-presentation review abstract, RNA-delivery study and T-cell activation teaching | Separates display from priming and retains the previously scoped model findings and direct-presentation explanation |

### Experimental-treatment access and planning, 11 November 2026

The [access-guide assessment](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/access-guides-2026-11-11/README.md) records the separate source versions, exact fields and reading limits. Changes are committed in [d49a0f58a](https://github.com/jasonLaster/OnCo/commit/d49a0f58aa230c53a5be44c3025a1ce2adf918d5), as unmerged draft contributions.

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-11 | [Placebo](/terms/placebo/) | The lead required every cancer-trial placebo to accompany standard treatment, while the summary ruled out blinding whenever adverse effects reveal treatment | Complete text of [FDA's August 2019 cancer-trial placebo and blinding guidance](https://www.fda.gov/media/130326/download), checked with NCI's trial explanation | Retains selected justified placebo-only settings and standard-care add-on designs; describes possible loss of blinding and qualified unblinding recommendations; corrects Simple and withdraws eight stale unreviewed machine summaries |
| 2026-11-11 | [Expanded access](/terms/expanded-access/) | A retained note made prospective IRB review an absolute rule for every subsequent institutional use | [FDA's April 2026 expanded-access guidance](https://www.fda.gov/media/162793/download), Q11 | States the normal prospective-review rule and FDA's qualified discretion for subsequent emergency use when prior review is infeasible, with the five-working-day reporting requirement |

The FDA submitted-request fraction is not used to rank supplier agreement as the usual bottleneck. That source assertion remains explicitly partial and unadopted. Readiness questions and fictional specimen arithmetic supply no individual eligibility, treatment date, predicted yield or combination-benefit claim. Older catalogue assertions outside the selected teaching retain their earlier source scope.

### Breast concept teaching, 11 November 2026

The [breast assessment](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/breast-concepts-2026-11-11/README.md) records the exact before/after fields and source depths. The corrections were applied in [d02cc0cc5](https://github.com/jasonLaster/OnCo/commit/d02cc0cc5e293d1f1d80639c47c125b995a13071) and [b8286149b](https://github.com/jasonLaster/OnCo/commit/b8286149b7f221c3620fe907374de8c63473e3db), as unmerged draft contributions.

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-11 | [Nucleus](/terms/nucleus/) | The short definition required the nucleus to be at a cell's centre | Source/field comparison and the [raw NCI definition](https://www.cancer.gov/publications/dictionaries/cancer-terms/def/nucleus) | Defines the membrane-enclosed chromosome compartment without requiring a central location; corrects simple English and withdraws stale short translations |
| 2026-11-11 | [Remission](/terms/remission/) | The summary generalised blood-cancer response criteria and a five-year cure proxy across cancers | Source/field comparison, the [NCI definition](https://www.cancer.gov/publications/dictionaries/cancer-terms/def/remission) and [NCI prognosis explanation](https://www.cancer.gov/about-cancer/diagnosis-staging/prognosis) | Defines partial and complete remission as observed changes without a universal cure timetable or eradication guarantee |
| 2026-11-11 | [ER](/biomarkers/er-status/), [PR](/biomarkers/pr-status/), [hormone receptor status](/terms/hormone-receptor-status/) and [TNBC](/cancers/tnbc/) | Leads equated ER-low with negativity or inferred treatment eligibility, benefit or personal risk directly from staining | Coordinator audit; CAP/ASCO guidance, RCPath passages and the [source receipts](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/breast-concepts-2026-11-11/primary-sources.json) | Keeps the validated categories, separate ER/PR observations, low-positive caveats and prognosis-versus-benefit distinction |
| 2026-11-11 | [HER2-positive](/terms/her2-positive/) | A dual-probe group was described as uniformly negative under US rules; test status, RNA subtype and treatment eligibility were blurred | Raw CAP testing rules and RCPath dataset passages | Retains concurrent IHC and indicated recounting, the attributed UK comparison and the separate medicine-evidence question; removes an unsupported treatment ranking |
| 2026-11-11 | [Germline testing](/technologies/germline-testing/), [gBRCA](/terms/gbrca-mutation/) and [germline BRCA readout](/biomarkers/brca-germline/) | Origin, family risk, tumour repair function and medicine access were conflated, with universal inheritance or sensitivity wording | NCI genetic-testing explanations, classification guidance and the source/field audit | Separates constitutional origin, family implications, assay coverage, current tumour function and indication-specific evidence |
| 2026-11-11 | [HRD in breast cancer](/terms/hrd-in-breast-cancer/) | Selected-study findings were generalised as prevalence or evidence of added platinum benefit | Resolved studies and the scoped assay/source comparison | Keeps assay-specific findings and study populations separate from treatment-benefit inference |
| 2026-11-11 | [Hereditary cancer syndromes](/terms/hereditary-cancer-syndromes/) | MEN1 and RET-associated MEN2 were conflated, and an MRI-alone causal mortality figure lacked support | NCI MEN table and selected raw explanatory passages | Separates the syndrome/gene relationships and withdraws the unsupported MRI-alone figure; other catalogue assertions retain their earlier source scope |
| 2026-11-11 | [Oligometastatic disease](/terms/oligometastatic/) | A small detected lesion count implied curability by treating each site | Consensus definitions and the scoped trial evidence | Separates detected burden, prior history, biological hypothesis and comparative treatment outcomes |
| 2026-11-11 | [Recurrence](/terms/relapse-recurrence/) | Timing implied deterministic biology and ctDNA monitoring implied a universal lead time | Historical cohort reports, selected methods/results and NCI explanations | Retains the population, event, clock, follow-up and conditional denominator instead of a personal deadline or universal monitoring interval |
| 2026-11-11 | [Curative intent](/terms/curative-intent/) | Intent was inferred from treatment intensity, modality or a universal disease-setting rule | NCI prognosis, chemotherapy, stage-based and palliative-care explanations | Separates the documented goal from observed response, medicine identity and eventual outcome |

These entries do not recertify every retained assertion in the touched records. The audit packet preserves abstract-only and selected-passage limits, the unchanged clinical catalogues and the absence of main/live, expert, learner or language approval.

### Therapy and conjugate teaching, 11 November 2026

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-11 | [Chemotherapy](/terms/chemotherapy-term/) | Leads reduced the family to DNA injury in fast-dividing cells | Raw product mechanisms and NCI definitions/purposes | Distinguishes DNA, RNA, metabolism and microtubule effects, exposure, recovery and clinical evidence |
| 2026-11-11 | [Alkylating agents and antimetabolites](/terms/alkylating-agent/) | Aliases and shorthand conflated fluoropyrimidines, vinca drugs and alkyl transfer | Current labels, terminology and canonical-owner census | Detailed fluoropyrimidine content stays on its drug owners; vinca aliases move to tubulin; the combined overview names the family boundary |
| 2026-11-11 | [Capecitabine](/drugs/capecitabine/) and [fluorouracil](/drugs/fluorouracil/) | Tablet-form shorthand, dated testing language and an unwarranted CREATE-X germline restriction | Current SPL/FDA text and resolved trial abstract | Distinguishes prodrug, host handling, complete/partial deficiency and the trial population; CAPOX/FOLFOX components remain separate |
| 2026-11-11 | [Anthracyclines](/terms/anthracycline/), [taxanes](/terms/taxane/) and [TOP1 inhibitors](/technologies/topoisomerase-inhibitors/) | Mechanism shorthand suggested inevitable death, fixed cardiac thresholds or comparative safety | Raw label sections, abstracts and targeted mechanistic passages | Keeps enzyme intermediates, interphase effects, exposure, source populations and uncertainty explicit |
| 2026-11-11 | [Antibody tumour penetration](/terms/antibody-tumor-penetration/) | No broad owner carried the transport and measurement teaching | Independent source inventory and named mouse/patient studies | Registers the parent without duplicating the narrower binding-site-barrier concept; universal distance and mass-ratio claims are not adopted |

The [assessment receipt](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/therapy-conjugates-2026-11-11/README.md) separates original/later inventories, current field evidence, reader/export checks and source-access residuals. Updated simplified summaries and English fallbacks prevent the older mechanism wording from surviving in reader layers. No main/live, expert or language sign-off is claimed.

Every factual correction to the OnCo corpus, newest first. Format: date · entity · what was wrong · how it was found · fix.

## 2026-11-11

### Tumour immunology and experimental scope

- **Pathway maps:** removed claims that an inflamed pattern guarantees PD-1 response, that a class-I-loss tumour is automatically killed by NK cells, or that fixing one cancer-immunity-cycle step predicts cure. Antigen presentation, recognition, tissue access and useful killing remain separate dependencies. The beta-catenin recruitment example no longer combines PTEN loss with an unrelated PIK3CA target.
- **NK and antibody mechanisms:** the receptor map distinguishes activating and inhibitory inputs, education and measured function. Margetuximab is described as Fc-engineered, rather than grouped as an afucosylated antibody. Its label's receptor-binding and NK-activation findings retain their in-vitro scope. The negative INTERLINK-1 comparison is kept beside earlier laboratory findings. Sources: [Long et al.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/23516982/), [MARGENZA label](https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=6a6d50c7-06ea-479c-83cc-f197a9e428c9), [INTERLINK-1](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/40300079/).
- **Interferon and CD4 readouts:** impaired interferon inducibility is separated from loss of all baseline recognition. STAT1, STAT2, STAT3 and IRF1 retain the melanoma experiments' actual scope. A FOXP3 stain alone does not establish suppression, and CD4 includes experimentally demonstrated cytotoxic states. Sources: [Zaretsky et al.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/27433843/), [Garcia-Diaz et al.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/28494868/), [Tran et al.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/17644734/), [Oh et al.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/32497499/).
- **Spatial and clinical interpretation:** tumour-location labels, stromal-area scores and counted-cell fractions have different denominators. Pancreatic mouse mechanisms no longer establish a rule for all human pancreatic tumours or the only possible responder group. Perioperative biomarker findings, anaesthesia trial endpoints and combined local/systemic treatment effects retain their separate populations and comparison limits.

The [tumour-immunology checkpoint](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/tumor-immunology-2026-11-11/README.md) records both source inventories, exact draft fields and source-access limits. These additions do not certify every retained historical claim, expert review, a named-language translation or main/live publication.

## 2026-11-10

### Pneumonitis attribution and product-specific labels

- **Interruption and discontinuation:** replaced a single generic grade ladder with the named product's instructions. The May 2026 trastuzumab deruxtecan table retains its separate adjuvant radiotherapy-only footnote; datopotamab deruxtecan and the October 2026 pembrolizumab label retain their own confirmation, severity and resumption conditions.
- **Monitoring and incidence:** removed the unsupported scheduled-imaging requirement and universal workup. The warning sections specify symptom monitoring and prompt investigation, including imaging for suspected disease. Naidoo's incidence estimates retain their two-institution cohort, denominators and observational scope.

The [source and field checkpoint](research/pneumonitis-labels-2026-11-10/README.md) records the targeted reading, current-label check and validation. This corrects one record; it does not close the broader pharmacology and toxicity curriculum audit.

### Effect measures and evidence interpretation

- **Statistical interpretation:** corrected the lead-time superlative, the p-value probability gloss, an inverted absence-of-evidence sentence and unsupported hazard-ratio examples. Frequentist intervals, equivalence, non-inferiority, competing risks and survival clocks now retain their different questions and assumptions. Fictional calculations are labelled as such.
- **Endpoint source scope:** STEEP 2.0's actual table distinguishes distant RFI from distant relapse-free survival. Aalen-Johansen is attributed to the reviewed technical vignette, separately from Austin's competing-risk tutorial. FDA endpoint guidance retains its December 2018, nonbinding scope. IDFS's existing check date is unchanged.
- **Surrogate examples:** corrected the historical olaratumab approval rationale and scoped the named trial comparisons. The TROPION-Breast01 account includes later antibody-drug-conjugate use. Unchecked legacy CodeBreaK 200, adjuvant-endpoint, MRD and UKCTOCS assertions are deliberately withdrawn from the summary and retained as named source follow-ups; related-study links remain.
- **Reader text and citations:** consolidated this batch's repeated notes while preserving published notes and unique source-backed teaching. Clinical benefit, assay signals, model outputs and personal predictions remain separate. DOI-cache additions record only individually resolved identifiers and preserve the broader cache date.

Source versions, exact fields, independent review findings and remaining work are recorded in the [statistics checkpoint](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/effect-reasoning-2026-11-10/README.md).

### Dose changes and CRS grading

- **Dose modification:** removed unsourced class-wide dose-reduction and discontinuation ranges, a universal 85% dose-intensity claim, and an implication that lower targeted-drug doses are generally equivalent. The record now defines its reference and time basis, gives labelled fictional arithmetic, and scopes the Zhang observational and Goetz exploratory analyses to their populations. Eight stale short translations use current English fallback; the accurate simple definition is retained. Sources: [Alberto, 1995](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/7626852/), [Zhang et al., 2018](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/29368311/), [Goetz et al., 2024](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41523-024-00639-1).
- **CRS grading:** the lymphoma record described grade 2 hypotension as responding to fluids. The 2019 consensus uses hypotension not requiring vasopressors. The correction preserves the vasopressin exceptions, oxygen-support criteria and the rule that subsequent grading can proceed without fever after treatment of established CRS. Other management and trial text is carried forward outside this focused verification. Source: [Lee et al., original consensus author manuscript, Table 2 and footnotes](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12180426/).

### Binder evidence and manufacturing quality

- **Cell-product identity and release:** an older definition and summary presented HLA identity and a generic rapid-sterility timeline as a release checklist. They now distinguish engineered-cell identity, chain of identity, viability, potency and product-specific testing. FDA's scoped 2020 short-shelf-life example does not become a universal early-release rule. Corrected short translations already present in the shared draft are preserved with the aligned English definition.
- **Peptide benchmark and vaccine regimen:** the 29-peptide Truex vendor comparison used deliberately difficult sequences. The Rojas study gave vaccine and atezolizumab to sixteen recipients, followed by chemotherapy in fifteen; the observed response is not attributed to vaccine alone.
- **Binder comparison scope:** Kinna's thermal and particle measurements are separated from predicted aggregation scores. Its xenograft used a human AML cell line in mice, and its single-donor limitation concerns preceding cell work. Single-domain shark binding fragments are distinguished from the full receptor.
- **Guidance identity:** FDA's final November 2026 potency guidance has a different landing URL from the retained December 2023 draft page. The final PDF and 3 November Federal Register notice support the current text; a content-current date is not treated as an issuance day. US manufacturer release and conditional official FDA lot release retain their separate scopes.

Exact fields, source identities and read-depth limits are retained in the [binder and manufacturing checkpoint](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/binders-cmc-2026-11-10/README.md).

### Innate immunity, cell identity and source scope

- **Macrophage states:** the retained TAM summary treated an M2-like label as the prevailing identity. It now explains that M1/M2 labels arise from laboratory stimulation and do not certify a tumour cell's state or suppressive function. The location name, measured programme and functional test remain separate. Sources and exact fields are in the [innate source reconciliation](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/innate-immunity-2026-11-10/primary-sources.json).
- **Recognition systems:** the immune-system definition replaces a rigid fast/generic versus slow/remembering division with inherited receptor systems versus receptors assembled during development, while preserving qualified trained-monocyte and memory-like NK findings. Chemokine activation effects and soluble complement defences are explicit rather than lost in the comparison.
- **Experimental scope:** the checkpoint additions keep the galectin-9/Bat3 binding result in the tested 293T setting, separate from the Th1-cell death result. The reviewed receptor-selection article is described as a review. Interferon protein amount, RNA score and controlled-exposure activity retain distinct readouts; receptor-family labels do not measure dose or potency.
- **Glossary routing:** singular and plural interferon lookup now belong to the dedicated interferon-family term; the broad cytokine definition remains linked. No ambiguous DCs alias is added.

### Breast anatomy, pathology and source scope

- **Ki-67 and monarchE:** the retained summary attributed the 20% eligibility threshold to cohort 1. It belonged to cohort 2; cohort 1 used its specified node, grade and size criteria. The current note separates trial eligibility from the FDA's 2023 removal of the testing requirement. MIB-1 remains a named assay antibody, rather than the definition of every Ki-67 index. Source: [FDA abemaciclib expansion](https://www.fda.gov/drugs/resources-information-approved-drugs/fda-expands-early-breast-cancer-indication-abemaciclib-endocrine-therapy).
- **p63 denominator:** the Werling series reported at least focal tumour-cell staining in 11% of cases, rather than staining in 11% of tumour cells. Cohort-specific observations are not universal diagnostic accuracy. Source: [Werling et al.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/12502930/).
- **Response definitions:** pCR is tied to its named specimens and endpoint. The FDA's two combined breast-and-node trial definitions differ on residual DCIS, while older studies also used breast-only endpoints. RCB retains protocol-specific tumour-bed and nodal measurements, its empirically fitted equation and class cutoffs; fictional equation arithmetic does not certify a calculator response or an individual recurrence probability. Sources: [FDA pCR guidance](https://www.fda.gov/media/83507/download), [Symmans et al.](https://doi.org/10.1200/JCO.2007.10.6823).
- **Grading and staging:** CAP grade fields retain their applicability conditions. Accessible raw RCPath G148 text supports the restored source-specific reporting and audit statements. Its UICC-versus-AJCC instruction is no longer stretched into a rule that all UK reports omit prognostic staging. The audit's earlier source-access failure was not reproducible.
- **Clinical comparison scope:** breast-conservation trials found no significant survival difference in eligible study populations; they did not prove exactly equal personal survival probabilities. TAM-01 compared low-dose tamoxifen with placebo, and COMET's monitoring result concerns selected low-risk DCIS and short follow-up. B-51's nodal-irradiation result is tied to its cT1 to T3, cN1, M0 to ypN0 population. Sources: [long-term surgery comparison](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/12393819/), [TAM-01](https://doi.org/10.1200/JCO.22.02900), [COMET](https://doi.org/10.1001/jama.2024.26698), [B-51](https://doi.org/10.1056/NEJMoa2414859).
- **Reader preservation:** source-backed anatomy, sampling and treatment context is restored with its qualifications. Forty-six stale short summaries now use current English fallback; existing unaffected text and review labels remain. The Spanish and Chinese full pCR examples are refreshed against the current English hash and attributed as model drafts. No new named language review is claimed.

### Sequencing, RNA measurements and updated TMB policy

- **TMB selection:** current tumour-agnostic pembrolizumab statements now use the FDA October 2026 traditional-approval criterion of at least 13 mutations per megabase, with the authorised-test, disease and treatment-setting conditions. The former 10-mut/Mb criterion and published study cutoffs retain their historical scope; no response guarantee or universal low-TMB contraindication is inferred. Sources: [FDA conversion and updated indication](https://www.fda.gov/drugs/cancer-approvals/fda-grants-traditional-approval-pembrolizumab-or-pembrolizumab-and-berahyaluronidase-alfa-pmph), [current KEYTRUDA label](https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=9333c79b-d487-4538-a9f0-71b91a02b287).
- **What sequencing establishes:** read count, independent molecules, local depth, breadth, mutation density, cell purity and inferred cancer-cell fraction keep their own denominators and assumptions. Tumour-only sequencing does not establish constitutional origin from a familiar gene or allele fraction. Sources include [Jones et al.](https://doi.org/10.1126/scitranslmed.aaa7161) and [the TMB harmonisation experiment](https://doi.org/10.1136/jitc-2019-000147). Numerical exercises are fictional, not clinical thresholds.
- **HLA source scope:** the [LOHHLA study](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.cell.2017.10.001) selected all-loci-loss or no-loss regions for a validation comparison; that selection does not establish typical regional frequency. Gene-specific HLA locations are verified against HGNC. DNA retention, RNA expression, surface protein and peptide display remain distinct findings.
- **RNA readers:** captured RNA, probe counts, expression scores, inferred mixture components and functional output retain their separate methods. Reverse transcription is a common workflow rather than a definition of every RNA chemistry. Sixty-three stale short summaries use current English fallback, while 47 unchanged short entries retain their text and positions; no new named language review is claimed.

### Spatial methods and model evaluation

- **Model evaluation:** a held-out 80/20 split is no longer described as a universal minimum. Internal, external, donor and intervention holdouts answer different questions, and choices made using evaluation results become part of development. Sources include [TRIPOD+AI](https://doi.org/10.1136/bmj-2023-078378) and the [single-cell perturbation benchmark](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41592-025-02772-6).
- **Protein measurements:** mass spectrometry does not measure every protein or directly establish cellular function, and it is not an exclusive route to phosphorylation information. Protein-derived peptides, phosphosite signals, model estimates, cell assignments and functional effects retain their own scopes. Sources include the [protein-inference methods paper](https://doi.org/10.1021/pr401072g) and the [breast proteogenomic study](https://doi.org/10.1038/nature18003).
- **Predictions and assays:** unqualified transfer and clinical-response claims are removed from inherited lead paragraphs. A virtual-cell model returning no response to a mutation-driven drug is not guaranteed to be correct. The [IC50-to-Ki methods paper](https://doi.org/10.1093/nar/gkp253) supports the assay-condition distinction; an unverified FFPE immunopeptidomics adaptation is explicitly withheld. Existing notes carry the worked examples and reading guides, and four stale short summaries use current English fallback without a new language-review claim.


### Repair, signalling and what the assays establish

- **MYC citation:** the inherited link labelled OMO-103 resolved to a glioma CAR-T trial. The DOI is corrected to the independently resolved [Garralda phase 1 OMO-103 study](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41591-024-02805-1), already represented by the existing paper owner. No clinical benefit is inferred from MYC abundance.
- **Repair:** the short definitions no longer equate a variant, a genomic scar or a damage signal with current repair failure or treatment eligibility. Classical end joining is not described as universally inaccurate, and paired repair disruptions are not described as universally lethal. The [original LST study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22933060/), [Telli study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/26957554/) and [selected resistance cohort](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/32245699/) retain their own methods, populations and endpoints.
- **Signalling:** receptor amount, phosphorylation, drug engagement, functional dependence and clinical benefit have distinct interpretations. The [RAF transactivation experiment](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/20179705/) and [WNT974 phase 1 study](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/33941878/) are scoped to their actual interventions and observations. The existing KEAP1 identity receives a handwritten override through the established generated-target precedence rule, preserving its catalogue fields and lung supplement; generated gene files are unchanged.
- **Source and reader boundaries:** the crosslink-repair review is labelled as a review. DNA-fiber calibration and numerical practice examples are explicitly protocol-specific or fictional. Unscoped legacy prevalence, treatment, price and turnaround assertions are replaced by the completed source-scoped explanations; a clinical landscape update is a separate queued task. Stale short translations use the current English fallback and receive no new language-review claim.

### RNA design, delivery and evidence boundaries

- **AAV:** the source audit distinguishes a named viral genome, packaged-genome measurements and usable coding capacity. A general self-complementary capacity figure is replaced with the original inverted-repeat experiment and its coding-capacity trade-off. The blanket claim that none of the cited studies concerns cancer is corrected: the T-cell editing study includes a mouse leukaemia model, while human cancer benefit is not established here. Sources include [McCarty 2001](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/11509958/), [McCarty 2008](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/18682697/) and [Eyquem](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/28225754/).
- **RNA carriers and constructs:** ionisable-lipid charge is qualified by lipid and environment. Entry, endosomal release, expression, integration, receptor recognition and useful activity retain their own assays and populations. A residual-DNA number from a review is not treated as a universal release specification; the [NEB IVT protocol](https://www.neb.com/en-us/protocols/evaluation-of-ivt-reaction-products?pdf=true) supplies the explicitly vendor-scoped UV measurement caveat.
- **Coverage and ownership:** repeated takeaways and source lists retain dispositions without earning extra teaching credit. The assertion that on-target/off-tumour toxicity had no owner is corrected after finding existing therapeutic-index and lineage-antigen aliases. Their broader class-safety claims remain queued for verification rather than being inherited as fact.

## 2026-11-09

### Cell death, stress and source-review corrections

- **Cell-death mechanisms:** apoptosis no longer owns the broad aliases “cell death” or “programmed cell death”. The assessed records distinguish mitochondrial/death-receptor routes, necrotic appearance, durable senescence and adaptive immune consequences. Immunogenic markers alone do not establish useful immunity; [consensus assay guidance](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/32209603/) retains that boundary.
- **Autophagy, ferroptosis and metabolism:** descriptions now keep assay, model and perturbation limits. They no longer turn a selected cell-state dependency into a universal cancer property or a metabolic perturbation into clinical benefit. [Horton’s transgenic comparison](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/9616204/) separates SREBP-relative effects and measured synthesis; [Freed-Pastor’s 3D cultures](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22265415/) retain the nonsterol rescue distinction. Oxygen-consumption calculations now name the [published ATP-rate correction](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30097494/).
- **Antibody, HLA, tertiary lymphoid structures and TAP:** independent source review found three overcredited requirements, an omitted scoped association, duplicated TLS prose and an imprecise TAP citation label. Exact fields now state that same-target antibody formats can differ, distinguish ectopic location from a developmental stage, retain the scope of breast/melanoma associations and identify the TAP1 experiment. The [primary HLA comparison](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/25754738/) keeps its specific haplotype and method dependence.


### Antigen presentation, antibody functions and gene definitions

- **Antibody, epitope, B cell, ADCC, Fc function and HLA typing:** the sixteen-lesson audit separates receptor binding, display, activation and completed function; removes antibody-format and Fc-silencing generalisations; and prevents broad HLA names from standing in for a resolved allele. Stale short-summary entries now use corrected English fallback.
- **B2M, HLA and Fc-receptor genes:** short definitions describe checked protein functions rather than treating cancer-catalogue roles as definitions of normal growth control. Dated catalogue assertions retain their original scope. The remaining generator/corpus issue is explicitly queued.
- **HLA-C:** the observed peptide share retains the cell-line and assignment-method limits reported by [Schellens and colleagues](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/26375851/), alongside [allele-dependent expression](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/28649982/). This audit does not establish a universal peptide fraction or clinical response rule.

### T-cell identity and exhaustion

- **T cell and T-cell exhaustion pathway:** the generic T-cell definition implied universal target recognition and killing, and the exhaustion description used absolute rescue and DNA-fixation wording. The 14-page public education audit separated identity, recognition, state, measured function and benefit; it also removed subset/state aliases from the generic owner and placed true aliases on the specific terms. Primary sources include [human class-II-dependent CD4 cytotoxicity](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/32497499/), [distinct exhaustion programmes](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/17950003/), [different exhausted subsets](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30778252/), [chromatin stability after blockade](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/27789795/) and [human clonal replacement](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/31359002/). Twenty-seven translated/simplified entries now preserve these limits. This is an automated source audit, not expert or fluent-language sign-off. The existing detailed intervention maps retain a separately queued evidence review.

### Cell manufacture, preservation and traceability

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-09 | [Allogeneic cell therapy](/technologies/allogeneic-cell-therapy/) | Said TRAC editing prevents graft-versus-host disease, combined distinct HLA/CD52 strategies and promised immediate availability with a general conditioning requirement | Resolved early UCART19 and Chen clinical reports, selected CALM methods/results and current registry identity | Separates source/location and immune directions, retains specific edits/cohorts/risks and removes guaranteed readiness or conditioning claims |
| 2026-11-09 | [Apheresis](/terms/apheresis/) | Described collection as skimming one cell type over a fixed duration | FDA starting-material guidance and NIH supervised-procedure consent example | Describes a variable white-cell-rich mixture, receiving acceptance and distinct counts/volumes; scopes access and citrate risks without a universal schedule |
| 2026-11-09 | [Cryopreservation and cell-therapy cold chain](/technologies/cell-therapy-cold-chain/) | Gave one freezing/storage process and promised that the living product arrives alive and correctly matched | Actual YESCARTA/KYMRIAH formulation/storage passages, scoped preservation experiments and traceability guidance | Retains product-specific conditions, viable recovery versus recovered-set viability, separate function and authorised excursion assessment |
| 2026-11-09 | [Cell-therapy orchestration software](/technologies/cell-therapy-orchestration-software/) | Said software guarantees correct on-time return, eliminates mix-ups and that named commercial systems are regulator-required | Raw FDA traceability passage and bounded ICCBBA identity/custody definitions | Describes controlled workflow support and separate identity, custody, quality and clinical decisions without a vendor mandate or guarantee |
| 2026-11-09 | [Autologous CAR-T manufacturing](/technologies/car-t-manufacturing-process/) | Equated each dose with one run and generalised culture time, release order, waiting time and current plant/vendor claims | Product-specific FDA process guidance and primary manufacturing/outcome reports | Separates collections, runs, lots, samples, doses, clocks and clinical attrition; removes the unsupported general timings and plant/vendor assertions |
| 2026-11-09 | Alternative summaries for the five owners above and [in vivo CAR-T](/technologies/in-vivo-car-t/) | Simplified and translated text could retain older source, freezing, workflow and manufacturing claims after English correction | Actual dictionary imports and each selected entry compared with the corrected concept | Corrects 47 existing reader summaries and one inline Chinese export; retains seven missing translations as explicit English fallback, without inventing language-review status |


### Synthetic cytokine receptors and omitted interactive teaching

| Date | Entity or source | What was missing | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-09 | [Synthetic cytokine receptors](/terms/synthetic-cytokine-receptors/) | No canonical distinction between receptor architecture, soluble cytokine, platform naming and separate functional readouts | Later lab delta, resolved primary/preprint abstracts, selected raw results and Enoda's raw sponsor page | Adds the scientific concept with ligand-dependent/independent examples, sponsor-only MIMIC naming, explicit preprint status and distinct activity/elimination/clinical boundaries; links the existing armoured-CAR owner |
| 2026-11-09 | Standalone CAR-T HTML lab and inline biology images | Markdown census omitted script-generated component/quiz teaching | Read the complete program and both light/dark diagrams; compare exact Git and decoded image bytes | Separate non-Markdown census with blob-bound scope reviews, reading/field checks and required closure; lab plus two PNGs reviewed per ref, 1,885 other roles per ref remain unclassified |

[Primary identities](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/cytokine-receptors-2026-11-09/primary-identities.json), [source scopes](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/cytokine-receptors-2026-11-09/source-scopes.json) and [reading receipts](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/cytokine-receptors-2026-11-09/source-reading.json) keep experiments, the 2026 preprint and sponsor claims distinct. Ten complete MED abstracts and eleven new discovery occurrences are screened. No raw source, lesson body or image is copied. The two 1,888-asset versions are identical but separately required; their counts are not added. [Reader checks](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/cytokine-receptors-2026-11-09/render-verification.json) pass for 172 owners/eight authored body parts; validate/typecheck/lint and 283 files/2,827 tests pass, with two existing skips. [Scope and open work](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/cytokine-receptors-2026-11-09/scope.json).

| Date | Entry | What was wrong | How found | Fix |

This checks one non-graph consumer, alongside 171 graph owners; it does not certify the whole directory or promise treatment. Validate/typecheck/lint and 283 files / 2,825 tests pass, with two existing skips. | 2026-11-08 | [Pathway activation state](/terms/pathway-activation-state/) | Said phosphorylation alone sets pathway state independently of protein amount | Primary site-regulation, ischemia and network studies | Requires a defined output and distinguishes modifications, abundance and RNA signatures |
| 2026-11-08 | [Agonist and antagonist](/terms/agonist-antagonist/) | Conflated partial agonism with tissue-selective modulation and used only one antagonist mechanism | IUPAC recommendations and expressed-receptor inverse-agonism study | Separates full/partial/neutral/inverse effects, binding and system context |
| 2026-11-08 | [RNA splicing](/pathways/rna-splicing/) | Said genes rather than RNA transcripts were cut and pasted and generalized mechanistic/clinical consequences | NHGRI exon/intron definitions and processing mechanisms | Defines RNA joins, untranslated exons and prediction-versus-product limits; removes unsourced generalized frequency and outcome shortcuts |
| 2026-11-08 | [Epithelial-mesenchymal transition](/pathways/emt/) | Assigned migration, stemness, efflux and resistance to all EMT states | Consensus terminology and primary hybrid-state/mouse reporter studies | Keeps coordinated properties, intermediate states, cell attribution and model limits |
| 2026-11-08 | [Clonality testing](/terms/clonality-testing/) | Equated one repeated receptor pattern with cancer and implied universal unique rearrangements | EuroClonality interpretation guidelines, targeted raw-PDF review | Separates ancestry patterns from diagnosis and retains biological/technical pitfalls |
| 2026-11-08 | [Immunoglobulin/T-cell receptor clonality](/terms/ig-tcr-clonality/) | Opposed clonal cancer to uniformly polyclonal normal responses | EuroClonality interpretation guidelines, targeted raw-PDF review | Allows reactive clonal patterns, multiple alleles and assay-limited negative results |
| 2026-11-08 | [Methylation-array beta values](/terms/methylation-arrays/) | Called a processed signal ratio a direct fraction of DNA copies with one methyl mark | Bisulfite and oxidative-bisulfite methods | Retains signal processing, 5mC/5hmC resolution and cell-mixture limits |

### Systematic public-education coverage audit: access and regulation

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-08 | [Companion diagnostics](/technologies/companion-diagnostic/) | Presented a diagnostic result as deciding whether a drug is right for an individual | FDA companion-diagnostic definition and intended-use guidance | Defines information requirements, intended use and interpretation limits; scopes the diagram label to the corresponding treatment |
| 2026-11-08 | [Companion diagnostic term](/terms/companion-diagnostic-term/) | Said a companion test is universally required before prescribing a particular drug | FDA companion-diagnostic definition and both-label guidance | Scopes the requirement to the corresponding use and separates the result from clinical suitability |
| 2026-11-08 | [Drug label and indication](/terms/label-indication/) | Treated label wording as defining reimbursement and mixed it with broader access conclusions | FDA off-label and marketing-application information | Separates indication, evidence, suitability and payment |
| 2026-11-08 | [Off-label use](/terms/off-label/) | Gave an unsourced prescribing proportion and an unqualified evidence comparison | FDA explanation of unapproved use of approved drugs | Defines the category and separates it from investigational products and evidence strength |
| 2026-11-08 | [Traditional approval](/terms/full-approval/) | Defined traditional approval only by direct demonstrated clinical benefit | FDA expedited-program guidance, surrogate-endpoint section | Includes validated surrogate endpoints and removes the universal survival-benefit implication |
| 2026-11-08 | [Fast Track and RMAT](/terms/fast-track-rmat/) | Included an unsupported eventual-approval proportion and treated distinct programs as equivalents | FDA expedited-program and RMAT criteria | Keeps designation criteria distinct from authorization and timing; routes PRIME aliases to the existing EU owner with a scoped explanation |
| 2026-11-08 | [Accelerated approval](/terms/accelerated-approval/) | Generalized example-based withdrawal language without retaining indication and regulatory-process context | FDA accelerated-approval program and expedited-program guidance | Retains indication-specific confirmation and applicable withdrawal procedures |
| 2026-11-08 | [Research use only](/terms/research-use-only/) | Generalized IVD guidance into legal requirements for every research model and website | FDA RUO/IUO guidance | Scopes the category to applicable intended-use and distribution rules |
| 2026-11-08 | [LDT rule](/terms/fda-ldt-rule/) | Assigned all LDT clinical claims one FDA-review status | Current FDA vacatur/restoration notice and CMS laboratory-quality materials | Separates test origin, laboratory oversight, product authorization and clinical evidence |
| 2026-11-08 | [Marketing applications](/terms/bla-nda/) | Called the review target a deadline with a promised decision and generalized application timing | FDA NDA, BLA, IND and expedited-program information | Separates submission, filing, review goals and approval |
| 2026-11-08 | [Expanded access](/terms/expanded-access/) | Gave a general review-time shortcut and omitted the new-IND versus existing-IND protocol distinction | FDA expanded-access questions and answers, April 2026, Q4 to Q9 | Adds route-specific criteria, calendar versus working-day rules, review duties and separate delivery dependencies |
| 2026-11-08 | [Federal Right to Try](/terms/right-to-try/) | Omitted parts of the eligible-product test and used unsupported access-rate/timing comparisons | FDA current Right to Try criteria and FAQ | Retains the complete product criteria and separates individual review, consent, reporting and voluntary supply |

## 2026-11-03

### Accuracy round 2: a census of the country pages and the 60 most-linked cancer pages

Every numeric or dated sentence on the live country pages (United States, United Kingdom, Germany, Israel, India, Japan, Russia, China and the research-ranking context file) and every figure-bearing sentence on the 60 cancer pages with the most inbound links was read against its cited source in raw text: 3,875 sentences, 3,402 with a checkable figure. Before these fixes 25.0 percent of country figures [23.1 to 27.1] and 20.8 percent of cancer-page figures [18.8 to 22.8] were wrong, stale, imprecise or had no source on the page; 4.2 and 1.2 percent were plainly wrong. Verdicts are in `docs/audits/accuracy/sample-2026-11-03-round2-sweep.json` and the rates in `ledger.json`. Where a fault recurred, every copy in the corpus was found and fixed, and four checks now hold the line: `src/data/burden-dated.test.ts`, `src/data/burden-sourced.test.ts`, `src/data/country-extras.test.ts` and `scripts/doi-exists.test.ts`.

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-03 | [Trastuzumab deruxtecan in HER2-low breast cancer, England](/cancers/tnbc-metastatic/) | Eleven sentences across the triple-negative and breast UK pages, the coverage table and the guideline map said NICE does not recommend trastuzumab deruxtecan for HER2-low disease (TA992, 2024) and that a rapid review had no date. NICE replaced TA992 with TA1197 on 7 October 2026, which recommends it within the marketing authorisation; the four-nation table still said patients in England, Wales and Northern Ireland could not have it | Accuracy round 2 full sweep found it on the triple-negative page and widened to every mention of TA992; the TA992 page ("This guidance has been updated and replaced by NICE technology appraisal guidance 1197") and TA1197 recommendation 1.1 read in raw text | England, Wales and Northern Ireland rows, the access row, approval notes, the guideline map stance and the coverage note now give TA1197 as the current decision and TA992 as the 2024 refusal it replaced |

## 2026-10-26

| Date | Entity | What was wrong | How found | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-26 | [Smoking cessation in cancer patients](/technologies/smoking-cessation-after-diagnosis/) | The summary called the 2021 Annals of Internal Medicine study "a randomised trial of intensive cessation support in the ELCAP and Moscow cohorts". It is a prospective cohort study of 517 current smokers with early-stage non-small-cell lung cancer at two Moscow hospitals, with no randomisation, no cessation intervention and no ELCAP patients. The sentence also said quitting improves survival "by around a third in cohort studies" without a source. | Resolved [10.7326/M21-0252](https://doi.org/10.7326/M21-0252) through Europe PMC (PubMed 34310171) and read the abstract during the precision follow-ups. | Rewrote the sentence from the abstract: hazard ratio 0.67 for death from any cause and 0.70 for progression, five-year overall survival 60.6 against 48.6 per cent, stated as an observational study with self-reported smoking. Named the authors and design in the link label. |
| 2026-10-26 | [Paganetti 2014 proton RBE review](/key-papers/paper-paganetti-proton-rbe-ijrobp-2014/) | One review, Physics in Medicine and Biology 2014 (DOI 10.1088/0031-9155/59/22/R419, PubMed 25361443), was held as two paper records, and the curated record's citation count belonged to the unrelated editorial its earlier wrong DOI pointed at (PubMed 25585778). | DOI resolved through Crossref; the 20 October DOI correction left the pair awaiting a merge. | Merged `paper-paganetti-phys-med-biol` into `paper-paganetti-proton-rbe-ijrobp-2014` with the dedupe tooling, which writes a permanent redirect, and replaced the stale citation count with the review's own. |

|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-20 | [Weekly electronic symptom reporting](/ideas/idea-acc-electronic-pro-monitoring-standard/) and [weekly symptom check-ins](/ideas/idea-moon-pro-monitoring-standard/) | Both ideas said that trials showed weekly symptom reporting makes people live longer, and one said the PRO-TECT trial improved overall survival. PRO-TECT, the largest trial and the one designed to test survival, found no difference (hazard ratio 0.99); two smaller trials had reported longer survival. | Re-read against the [PRO-TECT final report](https://europepmc.org/article/MED/41915775) and the [JAMA 2022 secondary outcomes](https://doi.org/10.1001/jama.2022.9265) during the protocol-upgrades round, and against the corpus record `rejuv-measure-epro-as-treatment`, which already said so. | Rewrote both TL;DRs and summaries to separate the confirmed quality-of-life, function and emergency-visit benefits from the unsettled survival question, corrected the plain-language line, and removed the eight machine translations that repeated the survival claim until they are retranslated. |

## Proposed by the update bot (pending review)

- 2026-09-16: 44 proposals drafted from the feeds (32 high confidence); review in [docs/proposals/latest.md](docs/proposals/latest.md). Nothing here is a confirmed correction until a human moves it above.
- 2026-09-24: PR 74 carried 21 EMA rows a person had to read; 6 were applied by hand (olaratumab, pixantrone, alitretinoin, cediranib, ensartinib, imp4927) and 15 left as no change (non-cancer medicines, a withdrawn biosimilar application, a pending re-examination); the table is in the PR comment.

## How corrections are logged

- Anyone can report an error via a GitHub issue or pull request.
- A correction entry names the entity, states precisely what was wrong, says how it was found (reader report, automated audit, fact-check job, congress update, reviewer), and links the fixing commit.
- The automated [audit](/audit/) and weekly registry fact-check surface candidates; only confirmed errors are logged here.

## Surface-targeting evidence and labels, 11 November 2026

The [surface-targeting correction record](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/surface-targeting-2026-11-11/CORRECTIONS.md) qualifies target/dependence, processing, PK and target-ranking claims. It corrects the SG human-serum release value against the 2020 notice, IDeate-Lung02 outcome hierarchy and the displayed tarlatamab cycle-1 schedule/monitoring. Source-specific assays, cohorts and model results replace universal safety, resistance and superiority assertions. All prior notes and source links remain in order; broader catalogue and mechanism-template gaps remain explicit.

The [current-main refresh](research/education-coverage-2026-11-08/main-refresh-b1d278-2026-11-11/README.md) retains upstream irAE aliases and makes the immune-side-effect lead name its organ examples and conditional management, supported by the label and prior guideline assessment. It does not change search ranking or its floors.

### Mesothelin healthy-tissue risk, 12 November 2026

Removed the inference that a presumed dispensable protein function means little risk from targeting mesothelin on healthy tissue. Normal mesothelial expression requires functional testing of the actual binder and engineered product; a positive tumour stain does not establish safety. The Tchou tissue survey and live-cell experiment retain their separate populations. Sources: [Tchou et al.](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/22418702/), [FDA CAR T-cell development guidance](https://www.fda.gov/media/156896/download).

### Cell-therapy time short summaries, 12 November 2026

Removed the unsupported typical three-to-six-week range from the vein-to-vein short lead and its simple-English entry. The same inherited clause was removed from the eight translated short definitions while preserving their existing definition wording. The interval and analysis population depend on the programme; manufacture, transport, testing and clinical readiness are different parts of the pathway. This is a source-scope correction, with no native-language review claimed. Source: [FDA CAR T-cell development guidance](https://www.fda.gov/media/156896/download).
